We all know this is the reason you are checking the coverage. You want to know what the top names in the game are playing so you can unleash it on your, hopefully unsuspecting, buddies at the local store. So here are the lists from everyone who finished 6-1, or above, in the first day of Grand Prix São Paulo.

#### Antoine Ruel

##### 

 ![](https://magic.wizards.com/sites/all/modules/features/wiz_bean_content_deck_list/icons/decklist_download.png)
